and
\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\Phi
is in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}D)
, then there exists an extra-logical rule:

\{\Pi_{i}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

,

and

. If instead
\mathsf{top_{r}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}B)=\varnothing
, then there exists an extra-logical rule:

\delta

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

and
{\bf S}=\bigcup_{i=1}^{m}{\bf S_{i}}\cup\{\{\neg C_{1}^{{\sf o}},\ldots,\neg C_{k}^{{\sf o}}\}\}
.

The same condition applies to any constrained conditional permission in
\mathcal{P}^{\sf d}
, replacing
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
with
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}

,
{\bf T^{\prime}}=\{\{\top^{{\sf p}}\}\}
and
{\bf S}=\bigcup_{i=1}^{m}\mathbf{S_{i}}\cup\{\{\neg C_{1}^{\sf p},\ldots,\neg C_{k}^{\sf p},\neg C_{1}^{\sf o},\ldots,\neg C_{k}^{\sf o}\}\}
.

(v_{op})

For any pair of extra-logical rules

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{m+1\leq i\leq n}

\delta

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi^{\prime}

with
m+n\geq 0
, if
\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\Psi
occurs in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi)\wedge(\bigvee\Phi^{\prime}))
without belonging to
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi))
,
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi^{\prime}))
or
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}W^{\sf o})
, then there exists an extra-logical rule of the form:

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq n}

\delta

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Psi

with
{\bf S}={\bf S_{1}}\cup{\bf S_{2}}
and
{\bf T}={\bf T_{1}}\cup{\bf T_{2}}
.

The same condition applies to any pair of extra-logical rules of the same form where
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
replaces
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
, with
W^{\sf p}
replacing
W^{\sf o}
.

(vi_{op})

For any pair of extra-logical rules

\{\Pi_{i}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Pi^{\prime}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

\{\Pi_{i}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{m+1\leq i\leq n}

\delta

\Pi^{\prime\prime}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi^{\prime}

with
m+n\geq 0
,
\Pi^{\prime}=\Pi_{1},\ldots,\Pi_{m}
and
\Pi^{\prime\prime}=\Pi_{m+1},\ldots,\Pi_{n}
, if
\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\Psi
occurs in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\to\bigvee\Phi)\wedge(\bigvee\Phi^{\prime}))
without belonging to
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi))
,
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi^{\prime}))
or
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}W^{\sf o})
, then there exists an extra-logical rule of the form:

\{\Pi_{i}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq n}

\delta

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Psi

with
{\bf S}={\bf S_{1}}\cup{\bf S_{2}}
,
{\bf T}={\bf T_{1}}\cup{\bf T_{2}}
and
\Pi=\Pi^{\prime},\Pi^{\prime\prime}
.

The same condition applies to any pair of extra-logical rules of the same form where
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
replaces
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
, with
W^{\sf p}
replacing
W^{\sf o}
.

(vii_{op})

For any pair of extra-logical rules

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

\{\Delta_{i}\mid\Pi\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{m+1\leq i\leq n}

\delta

\Delta\mid\Pi\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.59515pt\hskip-4.96877pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.96877pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.96877pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.96877pt\hskip 4.96877pt}$}\hskip-4.96877pt\hskip-5.59515pt\raisebox{6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime\prime}}$}}\hskip-5.59515pt\hskip 5.59515pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi^{\prime}

with
m\geq 0
and
\Delta=\Delta_{m+1},\ldots,\Delta_{n}
, if
\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\Phi^{\prime\prime}
occurs in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi)\wedge(\bigvee\Phi^{\prime}))
without occurring in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\bigvee\Phi)
,
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\bigvee\Phi^{\prime})
or
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}W^{\sf o})
, there exists an extra-logical rule of the form

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\{\Delta_{i}\mid\Pi\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{7.85669pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{m+1\leq i\leq n}

\Gamma,\Delta\mid\Pi\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi^{\prime\prime}

with
{\bf T}={\bf T^{\prime}}\cup{\bf T^{\prime\prime}}
and
{\bf S}={\bf S^{\prime}}\cup{\bf S^{\prime\prime}}
.

The same condition applies to any pair of extra-logical rules of the same form where
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
replaces
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
, with
W^{\sf p}
correspondingly replaced by
W^{\sf o}
.

(viii_{op})

For any extra-logical rule

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

\{\Pi_{i}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi

with
m\geq 0
and
\Pi=\Pi_{1},\ldots,\Pi_{m}
, if
\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}\Phi^{\prime}
occurs in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}(\bigvee\Phi)\wedge W)
without occurring in
\mathsf{top_{r}^{\#}}(\scriptsize\mathrel{\hbox{\raisebox{2.41112pt}{$\rule[-4.52083pt]{0.33452pt}{9.04166pt}\rule[-0.16725pt]{5.7277pt}{0.33452pt}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{-1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\hskip-5.7277pt\raisebox{1.37277pt}{\makebox[5.7277pt]{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\ast}$}}}$}}}W^{\sf o})
, there exists an extra-logical rule of the form

\{\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi^{\prime}

\{\Pi_{i}\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.48051pt\hskip-3.85413pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.85413pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.85413pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{i}}$}}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip 3.85413pt}$}\hskip-3.85413pt\hskip-4.48051pt\raisebox{6.86388pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{i}}$}}\hskip-4.48051pt\hskip 4.48051pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Theta_{i}\}_{1\leq i\leq m}

\delta

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Phi^{\prime}

The same condition applies to any pair of extra-logical rules of the same form in which
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
replaces
\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt\raisebox{1.0pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle$}}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip 0.0pt}\mkern 2.0mu}
, with
W^{\sf p}
correspondingly replaced by
W^{\sf o}
.

For any extra-logical rule
\delta
in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
whose conclusion is
{\sf O}
-labelled (
{\sf P}
-labelled), we define the label
\delta_{\mathcal{O}^{\prime}}
(
\delta_{\mathcal{P}^{\prime}}
) as follows:

(a_{op})

if
\delta
is generated in accordance with point
(iii_{op})
in Definition
3.6
, then
\mathcal{O}^{\prime}
(
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
, respectively) is
\Big\{\dfrac{B:C_{1},\ldots,C_{k}}{D}\Big\}
;

(b_{op})

if
\delta
is generated from extra-logical rules
\delta^{\prime}
and
\delta^{\prime\prime}
with labels
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}
and
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}
(
\delta_{\mathcal{P}_{1}}
and
\delta_{\mathcal{P}_{2}}
) in accordance with points
(iv_{op})-(viii_{op})
in Definition
3.6
, then
\mathcal{O}^{\prime}
is
\mathcal{O}_{1}\cup\mathcal{O}_{2}
(
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
is
\mathcal{P}_{1}\cup\mathcal{P}_{2}
, respectively).

For each
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
-derivation
\pi
we say that a constrained conditional obligation
\dfrac{B:C_{1},\ldots,C_{k}}{D}

belongs to
obl(\pi)
if and only if there is (at least) one extra-logical rule labelled
\delta_{\mathcal{O}^{\prime}}
which is applied in
\pi
and such that
\dfrac{B:C_{1},\ldots,C_{k}}{D}
belongs to
\mathcal{O}^{\prime}
.

Moreover, we say that a rule
\dfrac{B:C_{1},\ldots,C_{k}}{D}

belongs to
perm(\pi)
if and only if there is (at least) one extra-logical rule labelled
\delta_{\mathcal{P}^{\prime}}
in
\pi
and such that
\dfrac{B:C_{1},\ldots,C_{k}}{D}
belongs to
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
, or it belongs to
obl(\pi)
.

We employ
obl^{\prime}(\pi)
(
perm^{\prime}(\pi)
) to denote
obl(\pi_{1})\cup\cdots\cup obl(\pi_{m})
(
perm(\pi_{1})\cup\cdots\cup perm(\pi_{m})
, respectively) whenever
\pi_{1},\ldots,\pi_{m}
are the immediate subderivations yielding the premises of the lowermost extra-logical rules applications in
\pi
. We shall use

-

D_{\pi}^{\sf o}
to refer to the conjunction of the formulas in
concl(obl(\pi))
, and
E_{\pi}
to denote the conjunction of the formulas in
concl(obl^{\prime}(\pi))
;

-

D_{\pi}^{\sf p}
to refer to the conjunction of the formulas in
concl(perm(\pi))
, and
E_{\pi}
to denote the conjunction of the formulas in
concl(perm^{\prime}(\pi))
.

.

,

-labelled formulas (

{\sf o}

-labelled formulas, respectively).

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

\rho

of

and

Definition 3.9
.

. Then,
\pi
is a proof of
\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf\times}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta
if and only if any sequent

in

is sound – and a paraproof, otherwise.

Lemma 3.9
.

Let
\times\in\{{\sf O},{\sf P}\}
. The rule of safe External Weakening

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf\times}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

EW

A,\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf\times}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

is admissible in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
.

Proof.

We reason by induction on the height of the proof
\pi
of
\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf\times}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta
. If
h(\pi)=1
, the conclusion is immediate. Otherwise, we reason by cases over the last rule applied. If the latter is a structural or a logical rule, we simply apply the inductive hypothesis to one of the premises to conclude. The same argument holds whenever the last rule is extra-logical and the premises are
\times
-labelled sequents. The only non-trivial case arises if the last rule is an extra-logical rule
\delta
and the premises are
{\sf F}
-labelled sequents. To reach the conclusion, we apply safe
LW
to each premise and then apply
\delta
.
∎

Let
\star
vary over
{\sf f},{\sf o},{\sf p}
. For any set
\Gamma
of
\star
-labelled formulas, we use
[\Gamma]^{u}
to denote the set of formulas obtained from
\Gamma
by deleting labels.

Lemma 3.10
.

The rule of safe Deontic Implication

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

op

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 4.4927pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-4.4927pt\raisebox{6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-4.4927pt\hskip 4.4927pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

is admissible in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
, with
[\bigcup({\bf T}^{\sf o}\cup{\bf T^{\sf p}})]^{u}=[\bigcup(({\bf T^{\prime}})^{\sf o}\cup{({\bf T^{\prime}})^{\sf p}})]^{u}
and
[\bigcup({\bf S}^{\sf o}\cup{\bf S^{\sf p}})]^{u}=[\bigcup(({\bf S^{\prime}})^{\sf o}\cup{({\bf S^{\prime}})^{\sf p}})]^{u}
.

Proof.

By routine induction on the height of the proof of the premise, exploiting the fact that
\mathcal{W}^{\sf o}\subseteq\mathcal{W}^{\sf p}
and
\mathcal{O}\subseteq\mathcal{P}
.
∎

Lemma 3.11
.

Let
\times\in\{{\sf O},{\sf P}\}
and
\otimes\in\{\wedge,\vee,\neg\}
.
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
calculi enjoy the following structural properties.

(i)

The rules
R\otimes^{\times}
,
R\neg\otimes^{\times}
,
L\wedge
,
L\neg\vee
and
L\neg\neg
are invertible in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
.

(ii)

The rules
E\vee^{\times}
and
E\neg\wedge^{\times}
are admissible in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
.

(iii)

The
\times
-labelled versions of Left and Right Contraction are admissible in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
.

Proof.

(i)
We argue as in the proofs of Lemmas
3.2
and
3.4
.
(ii)
We proceed as in the proof of Lemma
3.7
.
(iii)
We argue as in the proofs of Lemmas
3.3
and
3.8
.
∎

Theorem 3.9
.

The rule of safe Cut

\Pi_{1}\mid\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta_{1},A

\Pi_{2}\mid\Gamma_{2}\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta_{2},\neg A

\Pi_{1},\Pi_{2}\mid\Gamma_{2},\Gamma_{1}\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta_{1},\Delta_{2}

is admissible in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
.

Proof.

We proceed as in the proof of Theorem
3.5
, exploiting Lemma
3.11
.
∎

Theorem 3.10
.

Let
\langle\mathcal{W},\mathcal{D}\rangle
be a default theory and
\langle\langle\mathcal{W}^{\sf o},\mathcal{O}\rangle,\langle\mathcal{W}^{\sf p},\mathcal{P}\rangle\rangle
be a normative system. Then the following statements hold.

(i)

\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
proves
\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}A
if and only if
A
belongs to (at least) one
d
-extension of
\langle\langle\mathcal{W}\cup\Pi,\mathcal{D}\rangle,\langle\langle\mathcal{W}^{\sf o}\cup\Gamma,\mathcal{O}\rangle,\langle\mathcal{W}^{\sf p},\mathcal{P}\rangle\rangle\rangle
.

(ii)

\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
proves
\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf P}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}A
if and only if
A
belongs to (at least) one
d
-extension of
\langle\langle\mathcal{W}\cup\Pi,\mathcal{D}\rangle,\langle\langle\mathcal{W}^{\sf o},\mathcal{O}\rangle,\langle\mathcal{W}^{\sf p}\cup\Gamma,\mathcal{P}\rangle\rangle\rangle
.

Proof.

For the proof of each statement, we proceed as in the proof of Theorem
3.8
– leveraging Lemma
2.2
.
∎

4.
Navigating doxastic and normative conflicts: examples

In this section, we examine a broad range of deontic scenarios using the proof-theoretic tools introduced so far. We show that controlled sequent calculi are flexible enough to capture deontic notions beyond plain obligations and permissions, and to address paradoxical or dilemmatic situations involving conflicting normative requirements. In certain cases, the
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
calculi are extended with appropriate extra-logical rules whose conclusions accord with our intuitive evaluations of the problematic scenarios in question.

To keep the presentation focused, the examples below omit explicit definitions of the underlying default theories and normative systems (we leave these details to the reader). When extending any
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
calculus with an extra-logical inference rule, we stipulate that additional extra-logical rules are simultaneously introduced to ensure that the closure conditions specified in Definition
3.6
continue to obtain. This stipulation guarantees that admissibility of safe Cut is preserved. For simplicity, we also assume that constrained obligations and permissions in the underlying normative systems are normal.

Example 4.1

(Typicality-based obligations)
.

Let the extra-logical rules

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{D}_{1}}

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

stand for the default rule ‘If Mary is an adult, then she is employed, unless she is a university student’, the conditional obligation ‘If Mary is employed, she ought to fill in an annual income-tax form’ and the conditional obligation ‘If Mary is a university student, she ought to pay the tuition fee’, respectively – where

(i)

and

;

and

;

{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf T_{3}}\cup\{\{r^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf S_{3}}\cup\{\{\neg t^{\sf o}\}\}
.

Consider the following derivations:

\delta_{\mathcal{D}_{1}}

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

where
{\bf V_{1}}=\{\{p^{\sf p}\}\}
,
{\bf U_{1}}=\{\{(\neg p\vee r)^{\sf f}\}\}
,
{\bf V^{\prime}_{1}}={\bf V_{1}}\cup\{\{q^{\sf f}\}\}
,
{\bf U^{\prime}_{1}}={\bf U_{1}}\cup\{\{\neg s^{\sf o}\}\}
,
{\bf V_{2}}=\{\{r^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf U_{2}}=\{\{\neg t^{\sf o}\}\}
. The leftmost derivation is a proof: if Mary is an adult, she is employed by default – and then she ought to fill in an annual income-tax form. The rightmost derivation is a proof too: if Mary is a university student, she ought to pay the tuition fee. On the other hand, the derivation in the middle is a paraproof: accordingly, if Mary is an adult and a university student, there is no obligation for her to fill in an annual income-tax form.

Example 4.2

(Practical syllogism)
.

Let the extra-logical rule and axioms

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

ax

q\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

stand for the unconditional obligation ‘One ought not pollute’ and the factual statement ‘If one rides a bicycle, one does not pollute’, respectively – where
{\bf T}=\{\{\top^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S}=\{\{\neg\neg p^{\sf o}\}\}
. In this scenario, a practical syllogism warrants the conclusion that there is an obligation to cycle. Consider the following inference rule for practical syllogistic reasoning:

\vdots

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

ax

A_{1},\ldots,A_{m}\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\Delta

prsyl

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}A_{i}

with
{\bf S}={\bf S_{1}}\cup\{\{\neg A_{i}^{\sf o}\}\}
,
1\leq i\leq m
and the side condition that
\overline{\overline{\mathsf{G4^{pn}}}}
refutes
A_{1},\ldots,A_{m}\vdash\Delta
. If we extend the
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
calculus for our scenario with
prsyl
, the following derivation becomes available:

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

ax

q\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

prsyl

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip-3.86632pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.86632pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.86632pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}}$}}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip 3.86632pt}$}\hskip-3.86632pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.86632pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

with
{\bf S^{\prime}}={\bf S}\cup\{\{\neg q^{\sf o}\}\}
. Such derivation is a proof. This corresponds to the fact that if there exists an obligation not to pollute and cycling contributes to avoiding pollution, there exists an obligation to cycle.

Example 4.3

(Chisholm’s paradox)
.

Let the extra-logical rules

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{3}}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg q

stand for the (un)conditional obligations: ‘The manager ought to review the budget’, ‘If the manager reviews the budget, then she ought to submit it for approval’, and ‘If the manager does not review the budget, then she ought not submit it for approval’, respectively
[
8
]
– where

(i)

{\bf T_{1}}=\{\{\top^{{\sf f}}\}\}
and
{\bf S_{1}}=\varnothing
;

(ii)

{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf T_{2}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf S_{2}}\cup\{\{\neg q^{\sf o}\}\}
;

(iii)

{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf T_{3}}\cup\{\{\neg p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf S_{3}}\cup\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\}\}
.

There exists an unconditional obligation to
p
, and the latter is equivalent to the negation of the condition of the norm captured by the rule – namely,
\neg p
. This corresponds to the scenario where there exists a primary obligation and a violation of the latter triggers a secondary obligation. The intuitive assessment is that the secondary obligation should hold, in spite of the fact that the primary obligation is violated.

To formalize such contrary-to-duty obligation, we require
{\sf O}
-labelled conclusions of extra-logical rules to be consistent with facts – that is, extra-logical axioms in
\mathcal{W}
, conclusions of defaults applied in the derivations of
{\sf F}
-labelled premises and factual assumptions in the repository. For any derivation
\pi
of an
{\sf O}
-labelled conclusion, we shall use
F_{\pi}
to denote the conjunction of formulas in
\mathcal{W}
,
concl(def(\pi))
and in the repository. Hence, we rewrite
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}},\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}
and
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{3}}
according to the following conditions:

(i^{\prime})

{\bf T_{1}}=\{\{\top^{{\sf f}}\}\}
and
{\bf S_{1}}=\{\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
;

(ii^{\prime})

{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf T_{2}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf S_{2}}\cup\{\{\neg q^{\sf o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
;

(iii^{\prime})

{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf T_{3}}\cup\{\{\neg p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf S_{3}}\cup\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}

Now, consider the following derivation:

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

ax

\neg p\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{3}}

\neg p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg q

R\wedge^{\sf O}

\neg p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\,p\wedge\neg q

Such derivation is a paraproof, whereas its immediate subderivations are proofs. Accordingly, if the manager does not review the budget, she ought not submit it – even though the primary obligation to review it still holds under no factual assumption.

Example 4.4

(Forrester’s paradox)
.

Let the extra-logical rules

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

stand for the (un)conditional obligations ‘One ought not kill’ and ‘If one kills someone, she ought to kill her gently’, respectively
[
11
]
– where

(i)

{\bf T_{1}}=\{\{\top^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S_{1}}=\{\{\neg\neg p^{o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
;

(ii)

{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf T_{2}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf S_{2}}\cup\{\{\neg q^{o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
.

Consider the following derivation:

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

ax

p\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

R\wedge^{\sf O}

p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p\wedge q

with
{\bf T_{2}}={\bf S_{2}}=\varnothing
,
{\bf T}={\bf T^{\prime}_{1}}\cup{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}
,
{\bf S}={\bf S_{1}}\cup{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}\cup\{\{\neg p^{\sf o}\}\}
. Such derivation is a paraproof, whereas its immediate subderivations are proofs. This corresponds to the fact that if one kills someone, she ought to kill them gently – in spite of the existence of an unconditional obligation not to kill anyone under the empty set of factual assumptions.

Example 4.5

(Specificity principle)
.

Let the extra-logical rules

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg q

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}r

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.59515pt\hskip-4.96877pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.96877pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.96877pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.96877pt\hskip 4.96877pt}$}\hskip-4.96877pt\hskip-5.59515pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.59515pt\hskip 5.59515pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

stand for the conditional obligations ‘If Fido is a dog, you ought not kill Fido’ and ‘If Fido is a dog and Fido is attacking a child, you ought to kill Fido’, respectively – where

(i)

{\bf T^{\prime}_{1}}={\bf T_{1}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{1}}={\bf S_{1}}\cup\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\}\}
;

(ii)

{\bf T^{\prime\prime}_{2}}={\bf T_{2}}\cup{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f},r^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime\prime}_{2}}={\bf S_{2}}\cup{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}\cup\{\{\neg q^{\sf o}\}\}
.

If Fido is a dog and is gravely attacking a child, one ought to kill him – an obligation that overrides the less specific obligation not to kill him merely because he is a dog
[
42
]
.

To formalize scenarios where the specificity principle applies, we compare obligations pairwise and require that the conditions of more specific obligations appear as factual constraints in less specific ones. Thus, we rewrite
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}
under the following condition:

(i^{\prime})

{\bf T^{\prime}_{1}}={\bf T_{1}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S^{\prime}_{1}}={\bf S_{1}}\cup\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\},\{r\wedge p^{\sf f}\}\}
.

Now, consider the following derivation:

ax

p\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

LW

r,p\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

r,p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.4472pt\hskip-3.4472pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.4472pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.4472pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf U}$}}\hskip-3.4472pt\hskip 3.4472pt}$}\hskip-3.4472pt\hskip-3.3875pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf V}$}}\hskip-3.3875pt\hskip 3.4472pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg q

with
{\bf V}=\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf U}=\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\},\{r\wedge p^{\sf f}\}\}
. Such a derivation constitutes a paraproof. This matches our intuitive assessment: if Fido is a dog and is posing extreme danger to a child, one ought to kill him, even though there is a weaker obligation not to kill him under the mere condition that he is a dog.

Example 4.6

(Extended Forrester’s paradox)
.

Let the extra-logical rules

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}^{\prime}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}^{\prime\prime}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}r

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{3}}^{\prime}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}r

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{3}}^{\prime\prime}

\Gamma\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-6.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{7.7956pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}q

stand for the (un)conditional obligations ‘There ought not be a fence around your house’, ‘If there is a fence around your house, there ought be a white fence around your house’ and ‘If you have a dog, there ought be a white fence around your house’, respectively
[
43
]
– where

(i)

{\bf T_{1}}=\{\{\top^{\sf f}\}\}
and
{\bf S_{1}}=\{\{\neg\neg p^{\sf o}\},\{r^{\sf f}\}\}
;

(ii)

{\bf T^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf T_{2}}\cup\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
,
{\bf S^{\prime}_{2}}={\bf S_{2}}\cup\{\{\neg(p\wedge q)^{\sf o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
;

(iii)

{\bf T^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf T_{3}}\cup\{\{r^{\sf f}\}\}
,
{\bf S^{\prime}_{3}}={\bf S_{3}}\cup\{\{\neg(p\wedge q)^{\sf o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
.

Consider the following derivations:

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

ax

p\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}^{\prime}

p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.593pt\hskip-5.593pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 5.593pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-5.593pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf U_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.593pt\hskip 5.593pt}$}\hskip-5.593pt\hskip-5.53328pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf V_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.53328pt\hskip 5.593pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

ax

p\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.593pt\hskip-5.593pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 5.593pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-5.593pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf U_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.593pt\hskip 5.593pt}$}\hskip-5.593pt\hskip-5.53328pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf V_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.53328pt\hskip 5.593pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p\wedge q

R\wedge^{\sf O}

p\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.4472pt\hskip-3.4472pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 3.4472pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-3.4472pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf U}$}}\hskip-3.4472pt\hskip 3.4472pt}$}\hskip-3.4472pt\hskip-3.3875pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf V}$}}\hskip-3.3875pt\hskip 3.4472pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p\wedge(p\wedge q)

where
{\bf V_{1}}=\{\{p^{\sf f}\}\}
,
{\bf U_{1}}=\{\{\neg(p\wedge q)^{\sf o}\},\{\neg p^{\sf o}\}\}
,
{\bf V}={\bf T_{1}}\cup{\bf V_{1}}
and
{\bf U}={\bf S_{1}}\cup{\bf U_{1}}
. It is easy to verify that both derivations are paraproofs, and that both subderivations in the rightmost derivation are proofs. This corresponds to the fact that (a) the unconditional prohibition of having a fence around your house is overridden by the obligation of having a fence in case you possess a dog, and that (b) when the unconditional prohibition of having a fence around your house is violated, a secondary obligation is triggered for you to have a white one.

Example 4.7

(Euthyphro’s dilemma)
.

Euthyphro is prosecuting his father for murder, despite the traditional belief that it is impious to prosecute or dishonor a parent. Let the extra-logical axiom and rules

ax

\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.04861pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{-6.25pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}$}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip-2.04861pt\raisebox{6.63889pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varnothing$}}\hskip-2.04861pt\hskip 2.04861pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p,q

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

\Pi\mid\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg q

stand for the factual statement ‘If one prosecutes her father for murder, one dishonors him’ and the unconditional obligations: ‘One ought to prosecute her father for murder’ and ‘One ought not to dishonor her father’, respectively – where
{\bf T_{1}}={\bf T_{2}}=\{\{\top^{\sf f}\}\}
,
{\bf S_{1}}=\{\{\neg p^{\sf o}\}\}
and
{\bf S_{2}}=\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\}\}
.

To formalize such dilemmatic scenario, we require
{\sf O}
-labelled conclusions of extra-logical rules to be compatible under the background of factual extra-logical axioms. Hence, we rewrite
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}
and
\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}
so that
{\bf S_{1}}=\{\{\neg p^{\sf o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
and
{\bf S_{2}}=\{\{\neg\neg q^{\sf o}\},\{\neg F_{\pi}^{\sf o}\}\}
. Now, consider the following derivation:

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{2}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{2}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg q

R\wedge^{\sf O}

\varnothing\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p\wedge\neg q

with
{\bf T}={\bf T_{1}}\cup{\bf T_{2}}
and
{\bf S}={\bf S_{1}}\cup{\bf S_{2}}
. Such derivation is a paraproof. This corresponds to the fact that Eutyphro cannot be obliged to prosecute his father and not to dishonor him at the same time.

There are noteworthy deontic notions that emerge from the non-trivial interaction among facts, (un)conditioned (constrained) obligations, and permissions. In the following examples, we approach these non-primitive deontic notions through the lens of controlled calculi. In certain cases, we allow control pairs to
decrease
in size along derivations, contrary to what happens under standard extra-logical rules in
\mathsf{G4^{\delta}}
calculi.

.

\Gamma\mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf F}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 3.14027pt\hskip-2.51389pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 2.51389pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-2.51389pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S}$}}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip 2.51389pt}$}\hskip-2.51389pt\hskip-3.14027pt\raisebox{5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T}$}}\hskip-3.14027pt\hskip 3.14027pt}\mkern 2.0mu}p

.

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{1}}

\delta_{\mathcal{O}_{2}}

and

;

\Pi\mid\ \mathrel{{}_{{}_{{\sf O}}}\mkern 2.0mu\rule[0.0pt]{0.4pt}{12.0pt}\hbox{\hskip 5.28606pt\hskip-4.65967pt\hbox{$\hbox{\hskip 4.65967pt\hskip 0.0pt\hbox{$\rule[0.0pt]{10.76385pt}{0.4pt}$}\hskip 0.0pt\hskip-4.65967pt\raisebox{-5.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf S_{1}}$}}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip 4.65967pt}$}\hskip-4.65967pt\hskip-5.28606pt\raisebox{6.80278pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf T_{1}}$}}\hskip-5.28606pt\hskip 5.28606pt}\mkern 2.0mu}\neg p

with

and
